<details><summary>🧠 思考过程</summary>

Now I'll implement v10: SESSION_TABLE gets parent_session_id, and migrateV10 adds the column via ALTER TABLE ADD COLUMN (simplest — no constraint change). But update SESSION_TABLE for fresh DDL. The comment "v10 要在这里加 parent_session_id" — with ALTER TABLE ADD COLUMN the migration is trivial and cheap. Project convention favors rebuild with frozen forms, but for "add a nullable column with no constraint", ALTER TABLE ADD COLUMN is the honest minimal action; rebuilding session table would require moving claims/probes FKs referencing session... Actually session is referenced by children with ON DELETE CASCADE; renaming session would break children references (they'd point to session_v9). That's a real reason to prefer ALTER TABLE ADD COLUMN. Good rationale for docs/findings.

Let me check how the SESSION_TABLE frozen form for v9 would be needed. With ALTER TABLE, no frozen copy needed. I'll document that decision.

Now implement:
1. schema.ts: SCHEMA_VERSION = 10, SESSION_TABLE includes parent_session_id TEXT, migrateV10 with ALTER TABLE session ADD COLUMN parent_session_id TEXT (guarding: if column exists skip).
2. repo.ts: SessionRow gains parentSessionId; insertSession accepts parentSessionId; getSession/listSessions select it. Add listSessions maybe include.
3. revisions / api: new endpoint.

Let's look at repo.ts insertSession and getSession.

<details><summary>🧠 思考过程</summary>

Wait: session has other tables referencing session(id) — claim, probe, event, etc. with ON DELETE CASCADE. When we RENAME session to session_v9, SQLite rewrites references in other tables to point to session_v9! That's the classic problem: with `PRAGMA legacy_alter_table=OFF` (default in modern SQLite), ALTER TABLE RENAME updates references in other tables' FK clauses. Then when we create new `session` table and drop session_v9, other tables' FKs point to a dropped table.

Hmm, but note rebuild() sets `PRAGMA foreign_keys = OFF`. Does that prevent the rewrite? No — the rename rewrite of references happens regardless of foreign_keys setting... Actually SQLite docs: "If foreign key constraints are enabled when a table is renamed, then any REFERENCES clauses in other tables that refer to the renamed table are also updated." When foreign_keys=OFF, the REFERENCES clauses are NOT rewritten. Yes! The docs state that the rename-references update only happens when foreign_keys is enabled. Since rebuild() turns FKs off first, other tables keep referencing `session`. Good — that's exactly why rebuild does that.

But careful — sqlite version behavior: docs say "If foreign key constraints are enabled when it is prepared, the ALTER TABLE RENAME command performs the rename and also updates references..." With FK off, no rewrite. OK.

Also DDL ordering: SESSION_TABLE is first in DDL, and after rename we re-create it via SESSION_TABLE. Fine.

But there's a subtlety with `PRAGMA user_version` and the test store.test.ts inserting `INSERT INTO session VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)` — 11 columns. Now session has 12 columns, so that will break. Those tests insert directly with VALUES. I need to update them to name columns. Let me check store.test.ts around those lines later.

Also `import` of SESSION_TABLE constant is defined before migrateV10? SESSION_TABLE is defined at line 36, module top-level, so yes fine.

Also DDL is derived from schemaStatements → includes SESSION_TABLE (new). Good.

Now update repo.ts to read/write parent_session_id.

<details><summary>🧠 思考过程</summary>

Now I need to build the compare service. Create `packages/server/src/compare.ts` with `compareSessions(db, id)` that:
- gets session; if null → null
- gets parentSessionId; if null/empty → { first: true } style result
- gets parent session; if missing → treat as first but note it
- build snapshots from listClaims + probes count (listProbes length? or total probes). Probes count = number of probes.
- call compareRuns

Return shape: `CompareView`. Also handle cycles: session whose parent chain visits itself. Simple guard: walk up max 20 hops; if repeat found, error.

Actually simpler: second session uses same RESUME text (accept revisions may change it but we can just pass same resume text). Compare uses claim text similarity across sessions. If extract returns identical claim text both times, similarity = 1.

<comparison.headline>

But those are pushed... and shift computed. Wait renumber(text, shift) - shift true, text '## 一、主张与判定' → bumpNumeral: numerals='一二三...', head = text.slice(0,1) = '#', indexOf('#') = -1 → returns text unchanged!

The bumpNumeral expects text starting with the numeral, but heading starts with '## '. Need to strip prefix. Fix: operate on the numeral inside.

Also '## 七、复现方式' appears — that came from repro numeral computed with shift? It gave 七, meaning shift false at that point? Actually applied.length is 0, shift true → numeral = applied>0 ? '八' : '七' = '七'. Right, correct outcome coincidentally. Hmm wait if shifted, no-side → 七. Correct.

So fix bumpNumeral: find the numeral at index 3.

Now design the web C1 feature.

What the user needs (C1: 简历版本对比 — "改完再跑一遍，判定怎么变"). UI design:

In RevisionBar (采纳改写栏), after 采纳, add a button 「改完再跑一遍」which:
1. Creates a new session with parentSessionId = current session id, same resumeText = revised.text (the revised resume), same jds/jobTitle/panel/difficulty/style, then runs it — basically re-runs the whole flow with the revised resume.

That's a bigger flow: it needs draft + panel etc. Simplest: App handler `handleRerunRevised` that calls createSession with `parentSessionId: sandbox.sessionId` and `resumeText: revised.text`.

Then after running, the new session's compare view is fetched and shown in a compare panel.

Also: for a historical session which has a parent (session.parentSessionId), show the compare panel too — fetch `/api/sessions/:id/compare` when loading.

UI component: `ComparePanel.tsx` — shows headline, improved/worsened/added/removed lists. Where to place? Maybe as a new Pane in right column above RevisionBar, or in the middle column. Hmm, screen real estate.

Let me think about the cleanest placement: The RevisionBar is in the right column bottom. After re-running, the compare belongs next to it. But if compare only appears conditionally, layout jumps.

Alternative: put compare in the StageRail (left column, process panel) — no, it's a conclusion.

Better: a new Pane in the right column, below RevisionBar? The right column has resume/jd grid + RevisionBar. Adding a third pane could make it cramped.

Option: Put the compare block INSIDE RevisionBar's Pane as a section at the top when compare exists? Conceptually "采纳改写" is about revising; showing "比上次" there is a bit off but it is adjacent to the workflow: 改完 → 再跑一遍 → 看变化.

Actually let me reconsider: maybe a better 4th location: the middle column has 3 rows (claims 3fr, requirements 2fr, counters 2fr). Could make compare a Pane that only renders when there is a compare... Grid rows would be dynamic — messy.

Hmm. Let me think about the flow the user sees: run #1 → verdicts → revise → "改完再跑一遍" → run #2 starts live in the same UI → when done, we want to show "比上次" prominently. A modal/drawer would be intrusive.

I think placing it in the right column directly above RevisionBar as its own Pane (only when there's something to show, i.e., compare != null and not first), and when nothing, render nothing. Layout jump is fine because it's an intentional reveal after re-run. Actually the right column is `flex flex-col gap-3`, so adding a pane just stacks it.

But wait — after re-running, `revised` is reset to null (new session), RevisionBar shows "勾选想采纳的建议" (since revised===null but sessionId != null). The compare pane would show above it. Good.

Let me design ComparePanel:

```
export function ComparePanel(props: { compare: CompareView | null; onShowOriginal?; busy? })
```
Hmm, we also need the "改完再跑一遍" button. Where? In RevisionBar, next to the export buttons: "改完再跑一遍" (data-shot="rerun-revised"). Enabled when revised !== null && revised.accepted.length > 0.

But careful: rerunning uses revised.text — the server has that. Actually simpler & more robust: server-side could compute. But createSession takes resumeText; we have revised.text locally. Fine.

Also need: parentSessionId must point to the session that produced the revision. Good.

Now, the flow of handleRerun: it's basically handleRun but with different resume + parentSessionId. Refactor: extract `runWith(input)` core? handleRun builds input from draft. Let me write a separate `handleRerunRevised` that duplicates the run dance... duplication is ugly (the run dance is ~60 lines).

Better: refactor handleRun into `startRun(options: { resumeText?: string; parentSessionId?: string })`. Let's look: handleRun uses draft.resumeText, draft.jds, jobTitle, runMode, runModel, panel, difficulty, style. For rerun: resumeText = revised.text (改完的简历), jds/jobTitle/panel/style/difficulty same, runMode same, parentSessionId = current session.

Also setDraft resumeText to the revised text? The intake panel shows the draft. If we re-run with revised text but draft shows original, confusing. So set draft.resumeText = revised.text too. Actually that's meaningful: 改完的简历成为新一场的输入，投料台上也该跟着变成改完的版本。

Let me implement:

```ts
const startRun = useCallback(async (input: RunInput) => { ... })
```
where RunInput = { resumeText: string; parentSessionId?: string }.

Then handleRun = () => startRun({ resumeText: draft.resumeText }) and handleRerun = () => revision!=null && startRun({ resumeText: revised.text, parentSessionId: sandbox.sessionId }).

Hmm but startRun must also update draft.jds etc. Fine, it sets material and dispatch begin.

Also after run completes, fetch compare: in the finally or after streamRun. Let's fetch compare inside startRun after streamRun: `const cmp = await fetchCompare(id).catch(()=>null); setCompare(cmp)`. And on handleLoadSession, also fetch compare.

Also on reset/clear, setCompare(null).

export type ShiftKind = 'better' | 'same' | 'worse' | 'unjudged';

export interface ComparedPair { prev: ComparedClaim; next: ComparedClaim; similarity: number; shift: ShiftKind; }
export interface VerdictCounts { judged: number; solid: number; weak: number; counts: Partial<Record<VerdictKind, number>>; }

export interface CompareView { sessionId; first; parentMissing; parentSessionId; parent: {id,jobTitle,createdAt,resumeHash}|null; resumeChanged; comparison: ClaimComparison|null }
export async function fetchCompare(id: string): Promise<CompareView>
```

Also CreatedSession needs parentSessionId. And CreateSessionInput parentSessionId.

- if first: don't render (or render nothing)

The button "改完再跑一遍" sets `runParent(sandbox.sessionId)` and immediately calls run with the current draft... but the current draft may be the original resume, not the revised one. Actually careful: `draft.resumeText` is the intake textarea. After a run with revisions accepted, the user's resume in the textarea is still the original. To rerun with the revised text, we'd need to put revised.text into draft.resumeText.

But `handleRun` reads from `draft`. Since it's a useCallback with deps including draft, calling setDraft then handleRun in the same handler would use stale draft. Simpler: add a param to handleRun: `handleRun(parentSessionId?: string, resumeOverride?: string)`.

Hmm, handleRun is called from IntakePanel `onRun={() => void handleRun()}`. I can change signature to `handleRun(options?: { parentSessionId?: string; resumeText?: string })`.

Let me design `handleRunAgain`:
```ts
const handleRunAgain = useCallback(() => {
  const id = sandbox.sessionId;
  if (id === null) return;
  const nextResume = revised !== null && revised.accepted.length > 0 ? revised.text : undefined;
  void handleRun({ parentSessionId: id, resumeText: nextResume });
}, [sandbox.sessionId, revised, handleRun]);
```
And in handleRun, `const resumeText = options?.resumeText ?? draft.resumeText;` and pass to createSession + setDraft/setMaterial accordingly.

Wait — but if revised text is used, the intake panel textarea still shows old text. Should we update draft.resumeText? The run sets `setMaterial({resumeText: draft.resumeText, ...})`. If we pass a different text, we should setMaterial with that text and also setDraft resumeText so the user sees what was actually run. Yes: `setDraft(current => ({...current, resumeText}))`.

Careful: run without revision accepted → parentSessionId with same resume → `resumeChanged` false → the report says "简历与上一版完全相同". That's an honest & useful outcome too (e.g., after changing difficulty).

/**
 * CrossExam 领域模型。
 *
 * 设计原则：**凡是 AI 说的话，都必须能指回简历原文的某个字符区间。**

```text
  const data = await getJson<{ sessions: SessionSummary[] }>('/api/sessions?limit=' + String(limit));
  return data.sessions;
}

export function getSession(id: string): Promise<SessionView> {
  return getJson<SessionView>('/api/sessions/' + encodeURIComponent(id));
}

export function fetchStats(sessionId?: string): Promise<LlmStats> {
  const suffix = sessionId === undefined ? '' : '?session=' + encodeURIComponent(sessionId);
  return getJson<LlmStats>('/api/stats' + suffix);
}

export interface CreateSessionInput {
  useDemo?: boolean;
  resumeText?: string;
  jdText?: string;
  jobTitle?: string;
  /** 这一场用什么跑；不传就沿用服务端启动时的模式。 */
  mode?: LlmMode;
  /** 联网时要用的模型名；回放模式会忽略它。 */
  model?: string;
  /** 这一场的面试官阵容：预设 id 或自定义 id，顺序即提问顺序。不传就用默认三位。 */
  interviewers?: string[];
  /** 1–8 份岗位描述（标题可省，省了取正文第一行）；不传或不给正文就等于没有 JD。 */
  jds?: { title: string; text: string }[];
  /** 追问强度：gentle / normal / hard。不传就是常规档。 */
  difficulty?: string;
  /** 改写风格：conservative / normal / bold。不传就是常规档。 */
  style?: string;
}

```text
/**
 * 采纳改写：前端这一侧的纯逻辑。
 *
 * 界面要回答三个问题，所以这里的函数也按这三件事组织：
 * 1. 我勾了哪几条？（勾选状态只是本地数组，服务端每次都从原文重算）
 * 2. 现在改到哪一步了？（已采纳 / 还没定 / 改不动，三个数分别数）
 * 3. 这次改动了多少？（改完的简历比原文长还是短，用户得看得见）
 *
 * 与 packages/server/src/revisions.ts 是两份代码、同一套语义：前端不能 import shared
 * （node:crypto 会打进浏览器包），所以类型在这里重复声明一份，靠测试对账。
 */

/** 一条落地的改写：区间是**原文**坐标，before 是原文该区间的逐字内容 */
export interface RevisionItem {
  claimId: string;
  start: number;
  end: number;
  before: string;
  after: string;
}

/** 被跳过的建议与理由（人话，直接显示） */
export interface RevisionSkipItem {
  claimId: string;
  reason: string;
}

/** 服务端返回的那一版改完的简历 */
export interface RevisedResume {
  text: string;
  digest: string;
  /** 已落地的改写，原文坐标 */
  applied: RevisionItem[];
  /** 同上，但是**新文本**坐标：给高亮用 */
  spans: RevisionItem[];
  accepted: string[];
  pending: string[];
  skipped: RevisionSkipItem[];
  updatedAt: string;
}

/** 勾选 / 取消一条建议。勾选顺序不影响结果：服务端每次都从原文重算。 */
export function toggleAccepted(current: readonly string[], claimId: string): string[] {
  return current.indexOf(claimId) < 0 ? [...current, claimId] : current.filter((id) => id !== claimId);
}

/**
 * 能勾选采纳的主张 id（顺序照界面上的主张顺序）。
 *
 * 有改写建议的算，用户自己补了一句真话的也算：补充说明走的是同一条
 * 「确认采纳」链路，如果这里不算它，补了说明却勾不上，那颗按钮点了没反应。

const VERDICT_THOUGHT: Record<VerdictKind, string> = {
  solid: '简历原文有这句话，追问也补上了口径与环境，两边对得上，不像是编的。',
  overstated: '数字本身在简历里，但两个口径被混着说，读的人会以为提升幅度更大。',
  unsupported: '这件事无法证伪，可简历只有结论、没有范围和贡献，撑不起这个说法。',
  contradicts: '回答与简历原文对不上，按原文为准。',
  unanswerable: '连续几次追问都答不上来，说明这句话背后的细节他自己也说不清。',
};

function verdictReasoning(prompt: string): string {
  const claimId = firstClaimId(prompt);
  const plan = planForClaimId(claimId);
  if (plan === null) return '';
  const kind = plan.verdict.kind;
  const parts: string[] = [];
  parts.push('先看这条主张：' + plan.text + '。');
  parts.push('追问记录里备了 ' + String(plan.questions.length) + ' 个问题，' + (plan.answer.answered ? '候选人有作答。' : '候选人没有答上来。'));
  parts.push(VERDICT_THOUGHT[kind]);
  parts.push('所以判「' + VERDICT_LABEL[kind] + '」，置信度 ' + String(Math.round(plan.verdict.confidence * 100)) + '%。');
  return parts.join('');
}

export function getSession(id: string): Promise<SessionView> {
  return getJson<SessionView>('/api/sessions/' + encodeURIComponent(id));
}

export function fetchStats(sessionId?: string): Promise<LlmStats> {
  const suffix = sessionId === undefined ? '' : '?session=' + encodeURIComponent(sessionId);
  return getJson<LlmStats>('/api/stats' + suffix);
}

  model?: string;

  interviewers?: string[];

  difficulty?: string;

  style?: string;
}

 * 版本对比的服务层（P11 · C1）。
 *
 * 纯计算在 packages/shared/src/compare.ts，这里只做三件事：
 * 把两场会话的主张从库里读出来、把追问次数数清楚、把链的合法性守住。
 *
 * 为什么不在 SQL 里做对比：对齐是一条纯函数，把它写进 SQL 就等于

    id: string;
    jobTitle: string;
    createdAt: string;
    /** 父会话的简历版本指纹：两份简历是否真的一样，只看这个 */
    resumeHash: string;
  } | null;
  /** 父会话的简历与这一场是不是同一份（改过就会不同） */
  resumeChanged: boolean;
  comparison: ClaimComparison | null;
}

import type { JobDescription } from './api.ts';

/**
 * 投料台上的「目标岗位 JD」列表。刻意与 shared 的 REQUIREMENT_LIMITS 同口径，

export const MAX_JDS = 8;
export const MIN_JD_CHARS = 40;
export const MAX_JD_CHARS = 6000;

export interface JdDraft {
  /** 可留空：留空时标题取正文第一行，与服务端的兜底同一套规则 */
  title: string;
  text: string;
}

export function emptyJd(): JdDraft {
  return { title: '', text: '' };
}

export function emptyDraftList(): JdDraft[] {
  return [emptyJd()];
}

/** 正文只有空白的行整行不算一份——粘错了留一行空格不该算成一份 JD */
export function filledJds(jds: readonly JdDraft[]): JdDraft[] {
  return jds.filter((jd) => jd.text.trim().length > 0);
}

/** 交给服务端的形状：标题原样，正文不 trim（原文区间要能对上用户看到的那段字） */
export function jdPayload(jds: readonly JdDraft[]): { title: string; text: string }[] {
  return filledJds(jds).map((jd) => ({ title: jd.title.trim(), text: jd.text }));
}

/** 只报第一个问题：一次说清一件事，比糊一屏更好改 */

  if (filled.length > MAX_JDS) {

import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { reportDocUrl, reportHtmlUrl, reportUrl } from '../src/api.ts';

/**
 * 三种导出形态的网址是给界面按钮直接用的，拼错了不会报错、只会静默 404，
 * 所以在这里钉住形状。
 */
describe('报告导出的网址', () => {
  it('三种形态各有一条网址，Markdown 是直接下载', () => {
    expect(reportUrl('s_1')).toBe('/api/sessions/s_1/report.md');
    expect(reportHtmlUrl('s_1')).toBe('/api/sessions/s_1/report.html');
    expect(reportDocUrl('s_1')).toBe('/api/sessions/s_1/report.doc');
  });

  it('print 参数只在要打印时出现', () => {
    expect(reportHtmlUrl('s_1', false)).toBe('/api/sessions/s_1/report.html');
    expect(reportHtmlUrl('s_1', true)).toBe('/api/sessions/s_1/report.html?print=1');
  });

  it('会话 id 会被转义，拼不出别的路径', () => {
    expect(reportUrl('a/../../etc')).toBe('/api/sessions/a%2F..%2F..%2Fetc/report.md');
    expect(reportHtmlUrl('a b', true)).toBe('/api/sessions/a%20b/report.html?print=1');
  });
});

/* ---------------------------------------------- 版本对比（改完再跑一遍） */

/** 对比里的一条主张：只要比判定，所以不带锚点与权重 */
export interface CompareClaim {
  id: string;
  text: string;
  category: ClaimCategory;
  /** 没判定是 null，不是 undefined：对比层只认「有没有判定」 */
  verdict: VerdictKind | null;
}

/** better 变好 / same 没变 / worse 变差 / unjudged 至少一边没判定 */
export type ShiftKind = 'better' | 'same' | 'worse' | 'unjudged';

export interface ComparePair {
  prev: CompareClaim;
  next: CompareClaim;
  similarity: number;
  shift: ShiftKind;
}

export interface CompareCounts {
  judged: number;
  solid: number;
  weak: number;
  counts: Partial<Record<VerdictKind, number>>;
}

export interface CompareSummary {
  threshold: number;
  pairs: ComparePair[];
  added: CompareClaim[];
  removed: CompareClaim[];
  before: CompareCounts;
  after: CompareCounts;
  improved: ComparePair[];
  worsened: ComparePair[];
  probesBefore: number | null;
  probesAfter: number | null;
  headline: string;
}

export interface CompareView {
  sessionId: string;
  first: boolean;
  parentMissing: boolean;
  parentSessionId: string | null;
  parent: { id: string; jobTitle: string; createdAt: string; resumeHash: string } | null;
  resumeChanged: boolean;
  comparison: CompareSummary | null;
}
```

Let me write:

```ts
export function shiftDetailOf(pair: ComparePair): string {
  const before = pair.prev.verdict === null ? '未判定' : VERDICT_LABEL[pair.prev.verdict];
  const after = pair.next.verdict === null ? '未判定' : VERDICT_LABEL[pair.next.verdict];
  return before + ' → ' + after + '（' + SHIFT_LABEL[pair.shift] + '）';
}
```
Mirrors shared shiftDetail.
